\DLine{AB01-PDF0211-body-L002}{}{}{dinem, latitudinem et altitudinem multiplicetur; invenietur magnitudo Martis 1 $\qfrac{1}{3}$ esse magni-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0211-body-L003}{}{}{tudinis Terrae, excepta modica quantitate haud computanda. — Amplitudo eius epicycli a}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0211-body-L004}{}{}{motu planetae inter primam et secundam stationem cognoscitur. Epicyclus quotidie 0° 31′ per-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0211-body-L005}{5}{}{currit; Mars autem in epicyclo 0° 28′ quotidie regreditur; illius motus igitur 0° 3′ remanet.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0211-body-L006}{}{}{Unde deprehenditur eum quandoque 5 menses, quandoque 6 in signo aliquo morari, ob varia-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0211-body-L007}{}{}{tionem eius motus apparentis; at re vera neque eius neque ceterorum planetarum motus cre-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0211-body-L008}{}{}{scit vel decrescit, immo semper unus et idem remanet. Diametrus autem epicycli Martis sub-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0211-body-L009}{}{}{tendit arcum 82° 21′ (1).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0211-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0211-body-L010}{10}{}{\Indent Magnitudinem Iovis perigei respectu ipsius magnitudinis in distantia apogea invenerunt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0211-body-L011}{}{}{ut 37 (2) ad 23 (3) esse, scilicet 1 $\qfrac{1}{2}$ + $\qfrac{1}{9}$ [1 $\qfrac{11}{18}$]; quod, in maximam Martis distantiam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0211-body-L012}{}{}{idest in 8022 multiplicatum, efficit Iovis longinquitatem apogeam 12924 (4) fere; qua re lon-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0211-body-L013}{}{}{ginquitas media 10473 (5) erit. Diametrum Iovis in distantia media invenerunt esse $\qfrac{1}{12}$ dia-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0211-body-L014}{}{}{metri Solis; si igitur distantia media per 12 dividatur, diametrus Iovis fere 872 $\qfrac{1}{2}$ + $\qfrac{1}{4}$}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0211-body-L015}{15}{}{[= 872 $\qfrac{3}{4}$] proveniet, quod, si cum 201 $\qfrac{1}{2}$ comparetur, cognoscitur perpaulo minus quam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0211-body-L016}{}{}{4 $\qfrac{1}{3}$ vicibus diametrum Terrae complecti. Hoc in longitudinem, latitudinem et altitudinem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0211-body-L017}{}{}{multiplicetur; Iovis magnitudo erit 81 fere vicibus ut magnitudo Terrae, et eius diametrus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0211-body-L018}{}{}{subtendetur arcui 8° 18′ (6) sphaerae caelestis. Ab intervallo inter primam et alteram statio-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0211-body-L019}{}{}{nem, et a motu epicycli, qui 0° 5′ (7) quotidie ad successionem signorum percurrit, — cum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0211-body-L020}{20}{}{eius motus diurnus apparens in inferiore epicycli parte 0° 54′ (8) ad occidentem versus sit, —}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0211-body-L021}{}{}{manifeste cognoscitur diametrum epicycli Iovis arcum 22° (9) subtendere.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0211-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0211-body-L022}{}{}{\Indent Variationem apparentem Saturni a minima ad maximam longinquitatem invenerunt esse}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0211-body-L023}{}{}{ut 1 $\qfrac{2}{5}$ ad 1, scilicet 7 ad 5 (10); e multiplicatione $\qfrac{7}{5}$ in distantiam apogeam Iovis proveniet}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0211-body-L024}{}{p. 185.}{igitur distantia apogea Saturni 18094 (11). Longinquitas media erit 15509 (12). Diametrum Sa-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0211-body-L025}{25}{}{turni in media longinquitate invenerunt $\qfrac{1}{18}$ diametri Solis esse; e media distantia, per 18}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0211-body-L026}{}{}{divisa, proveniet diametrus Saturni 861 $\qfrac{1}{2}$ + $\qfrac{1}{8}$ (13) circiter, quod si cum 201 $\qfrac{1}{2}$ diametri}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0211-body-L027}{}{}{terrestris comparetur, diametrus Saturni erit fere 4 $\qfrac{1}{6}$ + $\qfrac{1}{8}$ [= 4 $\qfrac{7}{24}$] vicibus ut diametrus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0211-body-L028}{}{}{Terrae. Hoc in longitudinem, latitudinem et altitudinem multiplicetur; invenietur magnitudinem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0211-body-L029}{}{}{Saturni fere 79 (14) vicibus magnitudinem Terrae complecti. Diametrus Saturni autem subtendit}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0211-body-L030}{30}{}{arcum 8° 12′ (15) sphaerae caelestis. Amplitudo eius epicycli ab intervallo inter primam et alte-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0211-body-L031}{}{}{ram stationem cognoscitur; epicyclus 0° 2′ (16) quotidie movetur, planeta in inferiore parte}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0211-body-L032}{}{}{epicycli 0° 57′ (17) ad occidentem versus percurrere videtur; diametrus epicycli igitur sub-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0211-body-L033}{}{}{tendit arcum 12° 26′ (18) (\textbf{\textit{b}}). \textit{Diametrus Solis subtendit arcum 39° 48′} (19) (\textbf{\textit{c}}).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0211-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0211-body-L034}{}{}{\Indent E stellis fixis 15 (20) dixerunt primae magnitudinis esse; earum distantiam fere 19.000}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}

\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0211-N02}{}

\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0211-N03}{}

\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0211-N04}{}

\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0211-N05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0211-notes_left-L005}{}{}{\Indent (5) Plato 10423.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0211-N06}{}

\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0211-N07}{}

\DLine{AB01-PDF0211-notes_left-L009}{}{}{Plato 0° 27′.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0211-N09}{}

\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0211-N10}{}

\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0211-N12}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0211-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (12) Cod. 15507; Plato 12209.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0211-N13}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0211-notes_right-L002}{}{}{\Indent (13) I. e. 861 $\qfrac{5}{8}$; sed melius 861 $\qfrac{1}{2}$ + $\qfrac{1}{9}$ =}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0211-notes_right-L003}{}{}{861 $\qfrac{11}{18}$.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0211-N14}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0211-notes_right-L004}{}{}{\Indent (14) Cod. 99.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0211-N15}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0211-notes_right-L005}{}{}{\Indent (15) Cod. 6° 39′; Plato 7° 39′.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0211-N16}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0211-notes_right-L006}{}{}{\Indent (16) Plato 0° 27′.}

\DLine{AB01-PDF0211-notes_right-L007}{}{}{\Indent (17) Cod. 0° 40′; Plato nihil habet.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0211-N18}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0211-notes_right-L008}{}{}{\Indent (18) Ita quoque Plato; cod. 32° 26′.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0211-N19}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0211-notes_right-L009}{}{}{\Indent (19) Plato 49° 48′; sed ambae lectiones error}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0211-notes_right-L010}{}{}{videntur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0211-N20}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0211-notes_right-L011}{}{}{\Indent (20) Plato 12.}

\DSource{0212}{123}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0212-body-L001}{}{}{semidiametros Terrae complecti, earumque magnitudinem $\qfrac{1}{20}$ magnitudinis Solis esse. Ipsarum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0212-body-L002}{}{}{distantia igitur, per 20 divisa, efficiet diametrum eorum siderum 950 (1); quae, cum diametro}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0212-body-L003}{}{}{terrestri comparata, dabit 4 $\qfrac{2}{3}$ + $\qfrac{1}{20}$ [= 4 $\qfrac{43}{60}$] diametros Terrae circiter. Hoc in longitu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0212-body-L004}{}{}{dinem, latitudinem et altitudinem multiplicetur; magnitudo harum quindecim stellarum erit}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0212-body-L005}{5}{}{fere 105 (2) vicibus ut magnitudo Terrae.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0212-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0212-body-L006}{}{}{\Indent Stellas fixas, quae in constellationibus reperiuntur, ad sex magnitudinis ordines posuimus,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0212-body-L007}{}{}{ab ordine quindecim (3) stellarum memoratarum decrescentes usque ad sextum ordinem, cuius}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0212-body-L008}{}{}{stellae 16 vicibus maiores sunt quam Terra. Omnium corporum maximum est Sol; post quem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0212-body-L009}{}{}{secundum locum obtinent quindecim stellae fixae primae magnitudinis; tertium Iupiter; quar-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0212-body-L010}{10}{}{tum Saturnus; quintum reliquae stellae fixae secundae magnitudinis [usque ad sextam]; sex-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0212-body-L011}{}{}{tum Mars; septimum Terra; octavum Luna; nonum Venus; decimum Mercurius, quod omnium}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0212-body-L012}{}{}{caelestium corporum minimum est.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0212-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0212-body-L013}{}{}{\Indent Si quis has diametros iterum metiri cupit, alhidadam sumat, cui duas pinnulas perforatas}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0212-body-L014}{}{}{aptet; foramen pinnulae ocularis parvum sit, alterius contra tale ut corpus totum sideris, nec}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0212-body-L015}{15}{p. 186.}{plus nec minus capiat. Quo facto, altera alhidada eiusdem magnitudinis Solem metiatur, et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0212-body-L016}{}{}{diametrum foraminis quo Solem metitus erit comparet cum diametro foraminis ad mensuram}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0212-body-L017}{}{}{stellae adhibiti. Necesse est comparationem eodem horizontis loco facere.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0212-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0212-body-L018}{}{}{\Indent Restat ut de quantitatibus stellarum loquamur, scilicet de arcubus sphaerae caelestis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0212-body-L019}{}{}{quos stellarum diametri in media distantia [a Terra] subtendunt, et exponamus quoque arcus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0212-body-L020}{20}{}{qui, iuxta mensuram factam, respondent distantiis apogeis et perigeis (\textbf{\textit{d}}). — Solis diametrum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0212-body-L021}{}{}{iam invenimus 39° 48′ (4) esse; eamque dicimus in omnibus distantiis ita se habere, nam quan-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0212-body-L022}{}{}{titate sensibili non variat. — Saturni diametrus in distantia apogea subtendit arcum 16° 22′ (5),}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0212-body-L023}{}{}{in media distantia arcum 36° 39′ (6), in minima arcum 33° 36′ (7). — Iovis diametrus in di-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0212-body-L024}{}{}{stantia apogea subtendit arcum 35° 36′ (8), in media arcum 14° 45′ (9), in perigea arcum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0212-body-L025}{25}{}{29° 36′ (10). — Martis diametrus in longinquitate apogea subtendit arcum 36° 10′ (11), in media}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0212-body-L026}{}{}{arcum 16° 14′ (12), in perigea arcum 33° 36′ (13). — Veneris diametrus subtendit arcum 0° 10′}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0212-body-L027}{}{}{in distantia apogea; arcum 0° 39′ (14) in media distantia; arcum 0° 36′ (15) in perigea. — Mer-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0212-body-L028}{}{}{curii diametrus subtendit arcum 0° 33′ in longinquitate apogea, arcum 0° 6′ in media, arcum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0212-body-L029}{}{}{0° 14′ (16) in perigea. — Lunae diametrum vidimus (17) in apogea longinquitate subtendi arcui}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0212-body-L030}{30}{}{0° 29′ 40″ (18), in media arcui 0° 39′ 36″ (19), in perigea arcui 0° 36′ 22″ (20).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0212-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0212-body-L031}{}{}{\Indent Inter has distantias memoratas, hae planetarum quantitates variant prout planetae amplius}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0212-body-L032}{}{}{vel minus [a Terra] absunt; idque aequationibus planetarum deprehenditur. Distantia sideris}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0212-body-L033}{}{}{ab apogeo vel perigeo epicycli cognoscitur ab aequatione media aequata ad quintam [i. e. apo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0212-body-L034}{}{}{gei] vel septimam [i. e. distantiae perigeae] columnam; distantia autem centri epicycli ab apo-}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0212-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0212-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Plato 920.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0212-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0212-notes_left-L002}{}{}{\Indent (2) Plato 102.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0212-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0212-notes_left-L003}{}{}{\Indent (3) Plato 12.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0212-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0212-notes_left-L004}{}{}{\Indent (4) Error ut in p. 122, l. 33; Plato 9° 48′. —}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0212-notes_left-L005}{}{}{Codicis numeros usque ad capitis finem servo, quos}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0212-notes_left-L006}{}{}{corrigere nequeo. Cfr. adnot. \textbf{\textit{d}}.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0212-N05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0212-notes_left-L007}{}{}{\Indent (5) Plato 6° 22′.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0212-N06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0212-notes_left-L008}{}{}{\Indent (6) Plato 7° 48′.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0212-N07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0212-notes_left-L009}{}{}{\Indent (7) Plato 8° 26′.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0212-N08}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0212-notes_left-L010}{}{}{\Indent (8) Plato 6° 0′.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0212-N09}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0212-notes_left-L011}{}{}{\Indent (9) Plato 248.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0212-N10}{}

\DSource{0213}{124}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0213-body-L001}{}{}{geo excentrici ex aequatione anomaliae deprehenditur. Per ambas has aequationes invenitur}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0213-body-L002}{}{}{longinquitas planetae a Terra, referta ad 60\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} quae semidiametrum efficiunt, sicut in capite de}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0213-body-L003}{}{}{distantiis Lunae cognoscendis a variatione eius motus [cap. XXXIX] docuimus.}
\par\vspace{4.42mm}
\par\fontsize{14.5}{17}\selectfont
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0213-H04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0213-body-L004}{5}{p. 187.}{\CenterLine{CAPUT LI.}}
\par\vspace{3.07mm}
\par\fontsize{14.2}{16}\selectfont
\DLine{AB01-PDF0213-body-L005}{}{}{De reperto motu stellarum fixarum, sive ipsae in sphaera moventur,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0213-body-L006}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{7.55mm}sive sphaera cum iis movetur; et de cognitione locorum stellarum}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0213-body-L007}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{7.55mm}per tabulas.}}
\par\fontsize{11.1}{13.8}\selectfont
\par\vspace{2.91mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0213-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0213-body-L008}{}{}{\Indent Dicit [auctor]: Status stellarum fixarum in earum ortibus, occasibus, culminationibus,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0213-body-L009}{}{}{moris super Terram et sub Terra, nec non earum a polo remotiones qualibet regione iam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0213-body-L010}{}{}{in hoc libro memoravimus. A tempore quo earum motus repertus est, hae stellae sine ulla}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0213-body-L011}{}{}{variatione circa polos eclipticae moventur, sed latitudinem suam semper servant; qua re di-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0213-body-L012}{15}{}{stantia alterius ab altera post tempus primae observationis nunquam variavit. Hac de causa}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0213-body-L013}{}{}{stellae fixae appellatae sunt, fixae scilicet in distantia ab aliis; nam omnes unum eundemque}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0213-body-L014}{}{}{motum habent, sive in eadem sphaera simul moventur, sive sphaera ipsa communis ab occi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0213-body-L015}{}{}{dente ad orientem, eas, ut sunt reliqui septem planetarum motus, motu suo transfert.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0213-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0213-body-L016}{}{}{\Indent Loca stellarum in longitudine et latitudine in libro Ptolemaei invenimus ad primum an-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0213-body-L017}{20}{}{num Antonini regis, qui est annus 886 (1) a Bukhtanaṣṣar primo [i. e. Nabonassar] rege. Una}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0213-body-L018}{}{}{ex observationibus stellarum qua Ptolemaeus (2) usus est, fuit observatio Menelai anno 845 (3)}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0213-body-L019}{}{}{Nabonassaris. Invenit tunc stellam borealem inter oculos Scorpii, quam sphaera armillari per}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0213-body-L020}{}{}{Lunam metitus est, in 5° 55′ (4) Scorpii esse; et iuxta ea quae Ptolemaeus in libro suo ex-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0213-body-L021}{}{}{plicat, eodem anno Cor Leonis in 2° 40′ Leonis, Sirius in 17° Geminorum esse debebant.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0213-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0213-body-L022}{25}{}{\Indent Nos has stellas fixas memoratas et alias quoque subsequentibus annis pluries observavi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0213-body-L023}{}{}{mus; et una ex observationibus quibus multum confidimus, anno 1191 a Dhū ’l–qarnayn (5)}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0213-body-L024}{}{p. 188.}{fuit; Lunam et culminationes stellarum observantes, declinationes et gradus signorum cum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0213-body-L025}{}{}{quibus culminant invenimus, unde etiam earum loca respectu eclipticae cognovimus. Patuit}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0213-body-L026}{}{}{igitur stellam borealem ex iis quae sunt inter oculos Scorpii occupare 17° 45′ (6) Scorpii, Si-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0213-body-L027}{30}{}{rium 28° 50′ Geminorum, et Cor Leonis 14° Leonis. Annus observationis nostrae fuit annus 1627}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0213-body-L028}{}{}{Nabonassaris regis; cum igitur ea 11° 50′ differentiae inter prima loca et loca nostri temporis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0213-body-L029}{}{}{dividantur per 782 (7) annos intervalli inter utramque observationem, inveniemus stellarum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0213-body-L030}{}{}{motum sex et sexaginta annis solaribus 1° attingere (8) (\textbf{\textit{a}}); et hoc modo tabulam motus stel-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0213-body-L031}{}{}{larum fixarum in annis collectis et singulis atque in mensibus descripsimus. Locis itaque in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0213-body-L032}{35}{}{libro Ptolemaei signatis nos 11° 10′ (9) addidimus (\textbf{\textit{b}}), ut stellas fixas ad annum 1191 post Dhū}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0213-body-L033}{}{}{’l–qarnayn describeremus; nullam contra differentiam sensibilem latitudinis invenimus in multis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0213-body-L034}{}{}{stellis quas observavimus. Tabulas igitur confecimus (10), quibus longitudines, latitudines, pla-}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0213-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0213-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Ita cod., Plato, et \Name{aṣ-Ṣūfī}; at deberet esse}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0213-notes_left-L002}{}{}{885.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0213-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0213-notes_left-L003}{}{}{\Indent (2) \textit{Almag.} VII, 3 (ed. Halma, t. II, p. 27).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0213-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0213-notes_left-L004}{}{}{\Indent (3) Plato 842.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0213-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0213-notes_left-L005}{}{}{\Indent (4) Plato 2° 22′.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0213-N05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0213-notes_left-L006}{}{}{\Indent (5) Incipit die 1 Sept. 879 post Chr. natum.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0213-N06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0213-notes_left-L007}{}{}{\Indent (6) Ita codex, ut videtur, bene; nam 5° 55′ Me-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0213-notes_left-L008}{}{}{nelai + 11° 50′ (cfr. infra, l. 31) = 17° 45′. Plato habet}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0213-notes_left-L009}{}{}{17° 20′, quod \Name{Halley}, \textit{Emendationes ac notae} etc.}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0213-notes_right-L001}{}{}{p. 920, emendare voluit 17° 50′. — Catalogus nostri}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0213-notes_right-L002}{}{}{auctoris, fol. 232,r., praebet 17° 40′.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0213-N07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0213-notes_right-L003}{}{}{\Indent (7) Plato 783.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0213-N08}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0213-notes_right-L004}{}{}{\Indent (8) I. e. quotannis 54″ 33‴; vide tabulam prae-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0213-notes_right-L005}{}{}{cessionis, in fol. 208,r. — Sed $\dfrac{\text{11° 50′}}{\text{782}}$ = 54″,4757 =}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0213-notes_right-L006}{}{}{54″ 28‴.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0213-N09}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0213-notes_right-L007}{}{}{\Indent (9) Cod. et Plato 11° $\qfrac{1}{2}$ + $\qfrac{1}{3}$ pro 11° $\dfrac{\text{1}}{\text{2}\times\text{3}}$.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0213-N10}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0213-notes_right-L008}{}{}{\Indent (10) Fol. 226,v. et seqq.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0214}{125}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0214-body-L001}{}{}{gas Caeli, et magnitudines stellarum fixarum continentur, ita ut earum loca post vel ante an-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0214-body-L002}{}{}{num 1191 ex ipsarum motu in tabula descripto cognoscantur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0214-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0214-body-L003}{}{}{\Indent Stellae, quas Ptolemaeus in Almagesto memorat, sunt 1022, praeter \textit{adh–dhu’ābah} (1),}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0214-body-L004}{}{}{\textit{al–fard} (2) et \textit{al–mirzam} (3) (\textbf{\textit{c}}). Earum magnitudines in sex ordines distributae sunt, quorum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0214-body-L005}{5}{}{primus est maximus, sextus minimus. Dicit quoque Ptolemaeus hasce stellas 48 (4) constella-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0214-body-L006}{}{}{tiones conficere, quarum 15 (5) ab austro zodiaci, 6 e figuris australibus zodiaci, — scilicet}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0214-body-L007}{}{}{Libram, Scorpium, Sagittarium, Capricornum, Aquarium, Pisces, — 21 (6) a septentrionibus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0214-body-L008}{}{}{zodiaci, 6 demum e figuris zodiaci borealibus. Loca constellationum zodiacalium variabunt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0214-body-L009}{}{}{progressu temporis quoad plagam Caeli, nam boreales fient australes, et australes boreales.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0214-body-L010}{10}{}{Stellae boreales zodiaci haec sunt: 13 Arietis e quibus \textit{ash–sharaṭāni} [i. e. duo \textit{sharaṭ}] in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0214-body-L011}{}{}{cornubus (7) et \textit{al–buṭayn} (8) in cauda; — 33 stellae Tauri, e quibus \textit{Pleiades} in cornu, et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0214-body-L012}{}{}{\textit{ad–dabarān} in dorso; — 18 stellae Geminorum, e quibus \textit{muqaddam adh–dhirā‘ayn} « an-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0214-body-L013}{}{p. 189.}{terior brachiorum » (9), \textit{al–haq‘ah} (10), et \textit{al–han‘ah} (11); — 9 Cancri, e quibus \textit{an–nath-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0214-body-L014}{}{}{\textit{rah} (12); — 27 Leonis, e quibus \textit{adh–dhu’ābah} (13), \textit{aṭ–ṭarf} (14), \textit{al–ǵabhah} (15) quae est}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0214-body-L015}{15}{}{\textit{Cor Leonis} (16), \textit{az–zubrah} (17) et \textit{aṣ–ṣarfah} (18); demum 26 stellae Virginis e quibus sunt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0214-body-L016}{}{}{\textit{al–‘awwā’} (19) et \textit{as–simāk al–a‘zal} (20).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0214-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0214-body-L017}{}{}{\Indent In constellationibus australibus zodiaci sunt: 8 (21) stellae Librae, e quibus \textit{al–ghafr} (22);}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0214-body-L018}{}{}{21 stellae Scorpii, e quibus \textit{az–zubānayāni} (23), \textit{al–iklīl} (24), \textit{al–qalb} (25), \textit{ash–shawlah} (26);}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0214-body-L019}{}{}{— 31 (27) Sagittarii, e quibus \textit{an–na‘ā’im} (28) et \textit{al–baldah} (29); — 28 Capricorni, e quibus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0214-body-L020}{20}{}{\textit{sa‘d adh–dhābiḥ} (30) et \textit{sa‘d bula‘} (31); 42 stellae Aquarii, e quibus \textit{sa‘d as–su‘ūd} (32) et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0214-body-L021}{}{}{\textit{sa‘d al–akhbiyah} (33); — demum 34 stellae Piscium, e quibus \textit{al–fargh al–muqaddam} (34),}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0214-body-L022}{}{}{\textit{al–fargh al–mu’akhkhar} (35) et \textit{ar–rishā’} (36) (\textbf{\textit{d}}).}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0214-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0214-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) I. e. Coma. — In \textit{Almag.} post finem cata-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0214-notes_left-L002}{}{}{logi (ed. Halma, t. II, p. 83), stellae dicuntur esse}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0214-notes_left-L003}{}{}{1022, iisque addendam esse \textgreek{ἡ πλόκαμος} « Comam »}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0214-notes_left-L004}{}{}{(tres stellas complectentem).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0214-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0214-notes_left-L005}{}{}{\Indent (2) Ita Arabes appellant \textgreek{α} Hydrae.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0214-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0214-notes_left-L006}{}{}{\Indent (3) Variae stellae ita appellantur; v. \Name{aṣ-Ṣūfī},}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0214-notes_left-L007}{}{}{p. 38, 95, 143, 221, 224.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0214-N04}{}

\DLine{AB01-PDF0214-notes_left-L011}{}{}{Ptolemaeum.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0214-N07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0214-notes_left-L012}{}{}{\Indent (7) I. e. \textgreek{β} et \textgreek{γ} Arietis. Auctor in sequentibus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0214-notes_left-L013}{}{}{stationes lunares Arabum describit, sed non semper}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0214-notes_left-L014}{}{}{recte, ut ex observationibus \Name{aṣ-Ṣūfī} p. 37-38 (quas}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0214-notes_left-L015}{}{}{in adn. \textbf{\textit{d}} refero) patet. Hodierna nomina stellarum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0214-notes_left-L016}{}{}{quae has stationes constituunt dedi ut sunt in indice}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0214-notes_left-L017}{}{}{\Name{Schjellerup} ad \Name{aṣ-Ṣūfī}; discrepantiae ab \Name{Ide-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0214-notes_left-L018}{}{}{\Name{ler}, \textit{Untersuchungen über den Ursprung und die}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0214-notes_left-L019}{}{}{\textit{Bedeutung der Sternnamen}, Berlin 1809, suis locis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0214-notes_left-L020}{}{}{indicantur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0214-N08}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0214-notes_left-L021}{}{}{\Indent (8) \textgreek{ε, δ, ρ} Arietis; \Name{Ideler} \textgreek{ε, δ, π} Arietis.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0214-N09}{}

\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0214-N10}{}

\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0214-N32}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0214-notes_right-L021}{}{}{\Indent (32) \textgreek{β} et \textgreek{ξ} Aquarii, et \textit{c} Capricorni; \Name{Ideler} \textgreek{β}}

\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0214-N34}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0214-notes_right-L024}{}{}{\Indent (34) \textgreek{α} et \textgreek{β} Pegasi.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0214-N35}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0214-notes_right-L025}{}{}{\Indent (35) \textgreek{γ} et \textgreek{δ} Pegasi; \Name{Ideler} \textgreek{γ} Pegasi et \textgreek{α} An-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0214-notes_right-L026}{}{}{dromedae.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0214-N36}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0214-notes_right-L027}{}{}{\Indent (36) \textgreek{β} Andromedae.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0215}{126}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0215-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0215-body-L001}{}{}{\Indent Stellae igitur constellationum zodiacalium sunt 346 (1); stellae 21 (2) constellationum bo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0215-body-L002}{}{}{realium, quae in catalogis memorantur, sunt 360; quae demum in 15 (3) constellationibus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0215-body-L003}{}{}{australibus reperiuntur, 316 (4); stellae omnes enumeratae igitur 1022, quarum 15 primae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0215-body-L004}{}{}{magnitudinis, 45 (5) secundae, 208 tertiae, 474 (6) quartae, 217 (7) quintae, 49 (8) sextae,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0215-body-L005}{5}{}{5 nebulosae et 9 (9) obscurae; quibus omnibus addendae sunt \textit{adh–dhu’ābah} « Coma », \textit{al–}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0215-body-L006}{}{}{\textit{–fard} et \textit{al–mirzam} (10). — E constellationibus extra zodiacum positis, stellas notavimus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0215-body-L007}{}{}{quibus notas qualitates in libro Ptolemaei invenimus (\textbf{\textit{e}}); praesertim magnas ex iis et ex stellis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0215-body-L008}{}{}{constellationum zodiaci. Earum status memoravimus, earumque vim cum vi Solis et Lunae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0215-body-L009}{}{}{comparatam.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0215-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0215-body-L010}{10}{}{\Indent Postea, propter stellas primae et secundae magnitudinis atque nonnullas tertiae, peculiares}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0215-body-L011}{}{}{tabulas (11) posuimus, in quibus stellarum declinationes, dimidias moras super Terram, altitu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0215-body-L012}{}{}{dines meridianas, et gradus signorum cum quibus oriuntur, culminant et occidunt descripsi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0215-body-L013}{}{}{mus, altitudine poli borealis super horizontem 36° sumpta, quae est latitudo urbis ar–Raqqah.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0215-body-L014}{}{}{Hi status stellarum ad annum 1211 (12) aerae Dhū ’l–qarnayn referuntur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0215-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0215-body-L015}{15}{}{\Indent In tabulis autem quae has praecedunt, longitudines stellarum ab initio Arietis supputavi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0215-body-L016}{}{}{mus. Si locum [i. e. longitudinem] cuiusvis stellae fixae in catalogo descriptae scire cupis,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0215-body-L017}{}{}{eius motum in annis post 1191 aerae Dhū ’l–qarnayn praeteritis adde longitudini catalogi;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0215-body-L018}{}{}{summam ab initio Arietis proiice, et quo numerando pervenies, ibi erit locus [i. e. longitudo]}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0215-body-L019}{}{p. 190.}{stellae in signo quod consecutus es. Latitudo autem, eius plaga, stellae magnitudo, manent ut in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0215-body-L020}{20}{}{catalogo describuntur. — Similiter e tabulis sequentibus gradus signorum qui cum stellis ascen-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0215-body-L021}{}{}{dunt, culminant, occidunt cognosces, si quod in tribus columnis reperitur proieceris ab Arietis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0215-body-L022}{}{}{initio, et post annum ad quem hi status stellarum referuntur addideris quantitatem conve-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0215-body-L023}{}{}{nientem numeris tabulae, nam magna est variatio quae omni tempore supputari debet. Hae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0215-body-L024}{}{}{res ad nostrum tempus descripsimus, ut modo veritati propinquo cognoscerentur; sed quo modo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0215-body-L025}{25}{}{observationibus deprehendantur iam in hoc libro docuimus. His tabulis quoque hoc nostro}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0215-body-L026}{}{}{tempore cognoscuntur novem figurae memoratae (13), quas stellae, praesertim maiores et in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0215-body-L027}{}{}{tabulis descriptae, cum Sole efficiunt.}
\par\vspace{4.48mm}
\par\fontsize{14.5}{17}\selectfont
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0215-H28}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0215-body-L028}{}{}{\CenterLine{CAPUT LII.}}
\par\vspace{2.89mm}
\par\fontsize{14.2}{16}\selectfont
\DLine{AB01-PDF0215-body-L029}{}{}{De cognoscendo id quod astrologi dicunt, nempe sphaeram caelestem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0215-body-L030}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{7.55mm}habere motum translationis prorsum et retrorsum; ac de manifesto}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0215-body-L031}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{7.55mm}errore huius sententiae.}}
\par\fontsize{11.1}{13.8}\selectfont
\par\vspace{2.87mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0215-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0215-body-L032}{35}{}{\Indent Dicit [auctor]: Narrat Ptolemaeus (\textbf{\textit{a}}) in libro suo, astrologos (14) tardum translationis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0215-body-L033}{}{}{motum, octogesimo quoque anno unum gradum attingentem, caelesti sphaerae tribuisse, et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0215-body-L034}{}{}{dixisse hunc motum usque ad 8 gradus directum et postea retrogradum esse. Significare illi}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0215-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0215-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) In enumeratione praecedenti sunt tantum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0215-notes_left-L002}{}{}{290, quia stellae extra figuras non computantur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0215-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0215-notes_left-L003}{}{}{\Indent (2) Cod. 18 ut supra; Plato 8.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0215-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0215-notes_left-L004}{}{}{\Indent (3) Plato 12, ut statim infra.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0215-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0215-notes_left-L005}{}{}{\Indent (4) Cod. 312.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0215-N05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0215-notes_left-L006}{}{}{\Indent (5) Plato 42.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0215-N06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0215-notes_left-L007}{}{}{\Indent (6) Cod. 174, Plato 414.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0215-N07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0215-notes_left-L008}{}{}{\Indent (7) Cod. 214.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0215-N08}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0215-notes_left-L009}{}{}{\Indent (8) Cod. 349.}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0215-N09}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0215-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (9) Cod. 8.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0215-N10}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0215-notes_right-L002}{}{}{\Indent (10) Cfr. notam 1 paginae praecedentis.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0215-N11}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0215-notes_right-L003}{}{}{\Indent (11) Fol. 237,v. et seqq.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0215-N12}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0215-notes_right-L004}{}{}{\Indent (12) Incipit, iuxta auctoris morem, die I Septem-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0215-notes_right-L005}{}{}{bris 899 post Chr. n. — Cod. 1251; Plato 1191; v.,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0215-notes_right-L006}{}{}{in secunda huius operis parte, adnotationes ad ta-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0215-notes_right-L007}{}{}{bulas foliorum 237,v. et seqq.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0215-N13}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0215-notes_right-L008}{}{}{\Indent (13) Cap. XLIX, p. 118–120.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0215-N14}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0215-notes_right-L009}{}{}{\Indent (14) \textit{Aṣḥāb aṭ-ṭilsamāt}, \textgreek{ἀποτελεσματικοί}.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0216}{127}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0216-body-L001}{}{}{volebant eclipticam ab occasu ad ortum, ut motus est stellarum fixarum, octo gradus per-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0216-body-L002}{}{}{currere; postea, in partem priori motui contrariam, scilicet ab oriente ad occidentem, rursus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0216-body-L003}{}{}{octo gradus perficere. Si res ita sunt, necessario primus translationis motus ab occasu ad or-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0216-body-L004}{}{}{tum debet motu stellarum fixarum procedere; id autem fieri nequit nisi ab alia re impellatur,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0216-body-L005}{5}{}{aut stellae fixae ipsae in eo moveantur, nam idem corpus non potest duos motus in duas con-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0216-body-L006}{}{}{trarias partes simul habere. Dixerunt quoque progressionem desiisse 128 annos Aegyptios ante}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0216-body-L007}{}{}{Augustum regem, scilicet anno 166 (1) Alexandri Macedonis; qua re, post eum annum, sin-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0216-body-L008}{}{}{gulis 80 (2) annis perfectis, unus gradus demendus esset usque ad octo graduum finem; tunc}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0216-body-L009}{}{p. 191.}{residuum addendum esset motui directo stellarum. Octo gradibus [iterum] perfectis, quod octo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0216-body-L010}{10}{}{superat addendum esset longitudini stellarum usque ad finem 8. graduum; tunc ad primum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0216-body-L011}{}{}{modum redire deberetur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0216-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0216-body-L012}{}{}{\Indent Anni spatium quo huius opinionis asseclae usi sunt, quantitate quintae horae partis, vel}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0216-body-L013}{}{}{quintae fere (3), 365 $\qfrac{1}{4}$ dies superabat (4) (\textbf{\textit{b}}); motus medius Solis igitur in anno Aegyptio}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0216-body-L014}{}{}{359° 44′ 43″ (5) esset. — Hipparchus, qui post eos vixit, anni spatium 365 $\qfrac{1}{4}$ dierum posuit,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0216-body-L015}{15}{}{qua re motus Solis in anno Aegyptio esset 359° 45′ 13″ (6); tamen putabat [fractionem anni}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0216-body-L016}{}{}{quae 365 dies excedit] esse minorem quam illam quartam diei partem. — Ptolemaeus (7), 285 (8)}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0216-body-L017}{}{}{annis post Hipparchum observans, invenit spatium anni esse 365 $\qfrac{1}{4}$ dierum, detracto $\qfrac{1}{300}$ diei}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0216-body-L018}{}{}{[i. e. 365\textsuperscript{\textit{d}} 5\textsuperscript{\textit{h}} 55\textsuperscript{\textit{m}} 12\textsuperscript{\textit{s}}]; motus Solis igitur in anno Aegyptio 359° 45′ 25″ (9) esse deberet. —}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0216-body-L019}{}{}{Nos autem 743 (10) annis post Ptolemaeum observavimus, et anni spatium invenimus 365 $\qfrac{1}{4}$}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0216-body-L020}{20}{}{— $\dfrac{\text{3 $\qfrac{2}{5}$}}{\text{360}}$ [i. e. 365\textsuperscript{\textit{d}} 5\textsuperscript{\textit{h}} 46\textsuperscript{\textit{m}} 24\textsuperscript{\textit{s}}] dies complecti, qua re motum Solis in anno Aegyptio 359°}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0216-body-L021}{}{}{45′ 46″ (11) esse comperimus. Hi motus omnes a tempore Nabonassaris crescunt; irritum ergo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0216-body-L022}{}{}{fit quod illi sentiebant de quantitate graduum, de amplitudine motus, de incremento et decre-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0216-body-L023}{}{}{mento. Sed nos hoc incrementum nullam rationem sequi videmus in retardando aut festinando;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0216-body-L024}{}{}{Ptolemaeus enim, 300 fere annis, unum diem circiter Hipparcho addidit; nos autem, 750 (12)}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0216-body-L025}{25}{}{fere annis, Ptolemaeo fere 4 $\qfrac{1}{2}$ dies addidimus, praeter diem quem ipse Hipparcho adiecerat.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0216-body-L026}{}{}{Si hoc ob errores evenit, qui in instrumenta haud bene divisa aut progressu temporis vitiata}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0216-body-L027}{}{}{irrepserint, necessarie etiam in observationes nostras post longum temporis spatium errores}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0216-body-L028}{}{}{incident, nam quae metiti sumus in observationibus nostris ad observationes illas referuntur.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0216-body-L029}{}{}{Si contra hoc ob aliquem sphaerae motum, quem nos et maiores nostri neque cognoscimus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0216-body-L030}{30}{}{neque an existat scimus, accidit, investigatio veri requirit ut omni tempore observationes fiant,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0216-body-L031}{}{}{et iuxta quod novi reperitur priores corrigantur, sicut qui antea fuerunt maiores suos emen-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0216-body-L032}{}{}{davere.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0216-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0216-body-L033}{}{}{\Indent Quod autem, ex observationibus factis, recte opinari possumus hoc est: cum augmentum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0216-body-L034}{}{}{illud in omnibus stellarum motibus appareat, hoc ob motum sphaerae stellarum fixarum eve-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0216-body-L035}{35}{p. 192.}{nire cogitandum est; Ptolemaeus enim, iuxta id quod suis observationibus invenit et secundum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0216-body-L036}{}{}{id quod iam ante eum factum est, dicit hunc motum unum gradum in 100 annis attingere;}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0216-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0216-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Plato 666. — Aera Alexandri Macedonis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0216-notes_left-L002}{}{}{vel ab Alexandro mortuo est aera Philippi Aridaei,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0216-notes_left-L003}{}{}{quam auctor noster incipit die 29 Aug. 324 ante Chr.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0216-notes_left-L004}{}{}{natum (cfr. adn. \textbf{\textit{a}} ad cap. XXXII).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0216-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0216-notes_left-L005}{}{}{\Indent (2) Plato 84.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0216-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0216-notes_left-L006}{}{}{\Indent (3) In textu Arabico pag. 191, l. 3, pro \ArabicRun{ونحوه}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0216-notes_left-L007}{}{}{codicis, qui procul dubio error est, \ArabicRun{او نحوه} lego. Plato}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0216-notes_left-L008}{}{}{\ArabicRun{نحوه} tantum habuisse videtur, vertit enim: « quan-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0216-notes_left-L009}{}{}{« titate quintae fere partis unius horae ».}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0216-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0216-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (4) Ergo 365\textsuperscript{\textit{d}} 6\textsuperscript{\textit{h}} 12\textsuperscript{\textit{m}}, vel haec quantitas fere.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0216-N05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0216-notes_right-L002}{}{}{\Indent (5) Secundae in cod. et Platone 48″.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0216-N06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0216-notes_right-L003}{}{}{\Indent (6) Cod. 359° 41′ 53″; Plato 329° 42′ 33″.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0216-N07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0216-notes_right-L004}{}{}{\Indent (7) \textit{Almag.} III, 2 (ed. Halma, t. I, p. 165).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0216-N08}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0216-notes_right-L005}{}{}{\Indent (8) Plato 282.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0216-N09}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0216-notes_right-L006}{}{}{\Indent (9) Minuta in cod. 44′; Plato 329° 42′ 22″.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0216-N10}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0216-notes_right-L007}{}{}{\Indent (10) Cod. 943.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0216-N11}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0216-notes_right-L008}{}{}{\Indent (11) Plato 329° 12′ 16″.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0216-N12}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0216-notes_right-L009}{}{}{\Indent (12) Plato 724.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0217}{128}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0217-body-L001}{}{}{sed inter observationes suas et antecessorum nimis breve spatium, 200 (1) annorum scilicet,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0217-body-L002}{}{}{praeterierat ut in hoc motu variatio manifesta reperiri potuisset. Inter Ptolemaeum autem et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0217-body-L003}{}{}{observationes nostras longum temporis spatium intercessit, et eum motum maiorem esse inve-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0217-body-L004}{}{}{nimus, ita ut 66 annis solaribus unum gradum perficiat (2). Ex horum motuum differentia}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0217-body-L005}{5}{}{illud augmenti provenit quod in omnes incidit.}
\par\vspace{4.56mm}
\par\fontsize{14.5}{17}\selectfont
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0217-H06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0217-body-L006}{}{}{\CenterLine{CAPUT LIII.}}
\par\vspace{2.96mm}
\par\fontsize{14.2}{16}\selectfont
\DLine{AB01-PDF0217-body-L007}{}{}{De temporibus revolutionum annorum eorumque ascendentibus, et de locis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0217-body-L008}{10}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{7.55mm}planetarum cum ad gradum quem initio occupabant revertuntur.}}
\par\fontsize{11.1}{13.8}\selectfont
\par\vspace{3.76mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0217-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0217-body-L009}{}{}{\Indent Dicit [auctor]: Si cognoscere vis revolutionem (\textbf{\textit{a}}) cuiuslibet anni nativitatum vel aliarum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0217-body-L010}{}{}{rerum exordium habentium, ad initium fixum, — et hoc est tempus quo Soli opus est ut ad}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0217-body-L011}{}{}{eundem gradum redeat quem, in illius rei initio, tenebat, — sume annum initii et annum aerae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0217-body-L012}{15}{}{Dhū ’l–qarnayn in quo vis revolutionem cognoscere. Minorem de maiore deme; remanet nu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0217-body-L013}{}{}{merus annorum perfectorum, qui ab initio constitutae rei vel a nativitate elapsi sunt usque ad}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0217-body-L014}{}{}{eundem diem mensis Romani in quo nativitas vel initium contigerant. Hos annos integros mul-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0217-body-L015}{}{}{tiplica in 86° 36′, quibus spatium anni dies perfectos excedit (3); de producto quot circulos}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0217-body-L016}{}{}{[i. e. 360°] potes deme; residuum per 15 (4) divide; exibunt horae aequinoctiales. Eas adde}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0217-body-L017}{20}{}{horis correctis originis; si summa minor est quam 24, eam cum die praeterito mensis serva;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0217-body-L018}{}{}{at si 24 excedit, de ea 24 deme et diebus praeteritis diem adde. Dies et horas ita inventos}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0217-body-L019}{}{}{serva. Si annus bisextilis est, atque iam mensis subāṭ praeteriit, de diebus elapsis diem deme,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0217-body-L020}{}{p. 193.}{et habebis dies correctionis; si autem annus non est bisextilis, dies ut invenisti serva, ipsi}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0217-body-L021}{}{}{enim erunt dies et horae correctionis. Per eos, ut docuimus, motum medium Solis anno dato}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0217-body-L022}{25}{}{cognosce; motus medius [i. e. longitudo media] Solis erit ut motus medius tempore originis.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0217-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0217-body-L023}{}{}{\Indent Aut, si mavis, annos repertos multiplica in 3° 24′, quae desunt ad complendam quartam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0217-body-L024}{}{}{diei partem excedentem 365 dies (5); de producto quoties potes 4 deme; si unus remanet,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0217-body-L025}{}{}{eum 90° (6) computa, si duo 180° (7), si tres 270° (8), si quatuor 360° (9). Ex eo quod fuerit}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0217-body-L026}{}{}{id quod servavisti deme; singulos quindecim gradus residui unam horam supputa, et horas}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0217-body-L027}{30}{}{provenientes adde, ut supra diximus, horis correctionis.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0217-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0217-body-L028}{}{}{\Indent Quod alterutra via invenis, idem est, scilicet aera; ad eam motum medium [i. e. longi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0217-body-L029}{}{}{tudinem mediam] Solis modo antea explicato, ut soles, determina; quod provenit corrige, et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0217-body-L030}{}{}{si locus verus Solis ut primus exit, est tunc tempus revolutionis. Si autem locum verum ori-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0217-body-L031}{}{}{ginis superat, ex motu horario Solis partem proportionalem ad portionem excedentem sume,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0217-body-L032}{35}{}{et de horis correctionis deme; si contra minor est, partem proportionalem motus horarii adde}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0217-body-L033}{}{}{horis correctionis; veraciter ita cognosces horas correctionis quibus numeras Solem usque ad}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0217-body-L034}{}{}{locum verum quem in origine tenebat.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0217-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0217-body-L035}{}{}{\Indent Per eas etiam locum Lunae et reliquorum planetarum corrige, easque in horas nychthe-}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0217-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0217-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Ita cod. et Plato; sed fortasse 280 legen-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0217-notes_left-L002}{}{}{dum, quod est intervallum inter Hipparchum et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0217-notes_left-L003}{}{}{Ptolemaeum.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0217-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0217-notes_left-L004}{}{}{\Indent (2) V. cap. LI, pag. 124, l. 33.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0217-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0217-notes_left-L005}{}{}{\Indent (3) Auctor annum tropicum 365\textsuperscript{\textit{d}} 5\textsuperscript{\textit{h}} 46\textsuperscript{\textit{m}} 24\textsuperscript{\textit{s}} esse}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0217-notes_left-L006}{}{}{invenit; multiplicentur igitur 5\textsuperscript{\textit{h}} 46\textsuperscript{\textit{m}} 24\textsuperscript{\textit{s}} in 15°, qui}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0217-notes_left-L007}{}{}{horam aequinoctialem perficiunt; exibunt 86° 36′.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0217-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0217-notes_left-L008}{}{}{\Indent (4) Plato 12,}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0217-N05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0217-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (5) Annus tropicus auctoris nostri 365 $\qfrac{1}{4}$ — $\dfrac{\text{3 $\qfrac{2}{5}$}}{\text{360}}$}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0217-notes_right-L002}{}{}{complectitur; ad perficiendam quartam partem diei}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0217-notes_right-L003}{}{}{desunt igitur 3° $\qfrac{2}{5}$ i. e. 3° 24′.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0217-N06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0217-notes_right-L004}{}{}{\Indent (6) Plato 60°.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0217-N07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0217-notes_right-L005}{}{}{\Indent (7) Plato 184.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0217-N08}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0217-notes_right-L006}{}{}{\Indent (8) Plato 274.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0217-N09}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0217-notes_right-L007}{}{}{\Indent (9) Plato 364.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0218}{129}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-body-L001}{}{}{merōn inaequalium hoc modo converte (1): aequationem dierum quae in tabulis ascensionum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-body-L002}{}{}{rectarum respondet gradui Solis, [de iis] deme, postquam cognoveris quota pars sint ex horis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-body-L003}{}{}{aequinoctialibus; provenient horae aequinoctiales pomeridianae, per quas, ut soles, ascendens}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-body-L004}{}{}{et paxillos deprehende. Haec variatio in loco vero Solis accidit ob motum eius apogei ab anno}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-body-L005}{5}{}{originis ad annum revolutionis; cum Sol prope apogeum aut perigeum consistit, variatio per-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-body-L006}{}{}{modica et insensibilis est; sed quo magis ab aliquo horum punctorum recedit, eo magis dif-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-body-L007}{}{}{ferentia crescit. — Patet quoque tempora revolutionum, 106 annis perfectis, diem originis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-body-L008}{}{}{una die antecedere (2).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0218-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-body-L009}{}{}{\Indent Ad tempora mediarum revolutionum annorum atque ad motum medium planetarum in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-body-L010}{10}{}{iis, tabulas confecimus (3), quarum usum in ultimo capitum post finem libri explicavimus (4),}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-body-L011}{}{}{ut facile adhiberi possint quotiescumque iis uti necesse est.}
\par\vspace{4.52mm}
\par\fontsize{14.5}{17}\selectfont
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0218-H12}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-body-L012}{}{p 191.}{\CenterLine{CAPUT LIV.}}
\par\vspace{3.03mm}
\par\fontsize{14.2}{16}\selectfont
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0218-H13}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-body-L013}{15}{}{\CenterLine{De veris quantitatibus applicationum [astrologicarum], quae secundum}}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0218-H14}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-body-L014}{}{}{\CenterLine{stellarum latitudines eveniunt.}}
\par\fontsize{11.1}{13.8}\selectfont
\par\vspace{3.67mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0218-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-body-L015}{}{}{\Indent Dicit [auctor]: Cum ecliptica in duodecim signa dividatur, duodecim autem nullum nu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-body-L016}{}{}{merum integrum multipliciter contineat praeter bis sex, ter quatuor, quater tres, et sexies}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-body-L017}{20}{}{duo, hae quantitates tantum adhibentur, quae quatuor \textit{aspectus} (5), praeter coniunctionem,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-body-L018}{}{}{efficiunt. Ex his aspectibus \textit{oppositio} (6) est, quae 6 signa, videlicet duos angulos rectos seu}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-body-L019}{}{}{180° complectitur; eius fortitudinis causa per se ipsam manifesta est. Secundus aspectus \textit{qua-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-body-L020}{}{}{\textit{dratura} (7) vocatur, et est dimidia pars oppositionis; tria signa, videlicet unum angulum re-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-body-L021}{}{}{ctum seu 90° complectitur. Postea aspectus \textit{trinus} (8), quatuor signa, videlicet 1 $\qfrac{1}{3}$ angulum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-body-L022}{25}{}{rectum seu 120° complectens; demum aspectus \textit{sextilis} (9), trini dimidia pars, quo duo signa,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-body-L023}{}{}{videlicet $\qfrac{2}{3}$ anguli recti seu 60° continentur. \textit{Coniunctionis} casui autem nulla est figura (10).}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-body-L024}{}{}{Illi gradus eclipticae nonnisi in aspectibus coniunguntur; signa quae tunc consociantur \textit{co-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-body-L025}{}{}{\textit{gnata} (11) dicuntur; reliqua vero signa, nunquam in aspectibus inter se colligata, sunt quae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-body-L026}{}{}{inter se uno signo, aut quinque (12) aut septem signis (13) distant.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0218-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-body-L027}{30}{}{\Indent Radii stellarum, quae in aliquo quatuor aspectuum consociatae sunt, in centrum Terrae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-body-L028}{}{}{conveniunt; qua re latitudines stellarum notare tunc supervacaneum est. Oportet contra lati-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-body-L029}{}{}{tudines in coniunctione observare (14); nam, si duae stellae, latitudines inaequales et in duabus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-body-L030}{}{}{oppositis plagis habentes, corporaliter congrediantur, coniunctio non est vera, nec inferior stel-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-body-L031}{}{}{larum superiorem occultat. Vera coniunctio, quam memoravimus, in longitudine et latitudine}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0218-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) I. e. tempus medium in verum converte.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0218-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-notes_left-L002}{}{}{\Indent (2) Nam annus Romanus 365 $\qfrac{1}{4}$ dies comple-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-notes_left-L003}{}{}{ctitur, annus tropicus auctoris nostri 365\textsuperscript{\textit{d}} 5\textsuperscript{\textit{h}} 46\textsuperscript{\textit{m}} 24\textsuperscript{\textit{s}};}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-notes_left-L004}{}{}{et si differentia 13\textsuperscript{\textit{m}} 36\textsuperscript{\textit{s}} in 106 annos multiplicatur,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-notes_left-L005}{}{}{proveniunt 24\textsuperscript{\textit{h}} 1\textsuperscript{\textit{m}} 36\textsuperscript{\textit{s}}.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0218-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-notes_left-L006}{}{}{\Indent (3) Fol. 239,v.-240,r.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0218-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-notes_left-L007}{}{}{\Indent (4) Cap. E.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0218-N05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-notes_left-L008}{}{}{\Indent (5) Apud Graecos \textgreek{σχήματα, σχηματισμοί, συσχη}-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-notes_left-L009}{}{}{\textgreek{ματισμοί} i. e. configurationes; sed interdum quoque}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-notes_left-L010}{}{}{\textgreek{ὄψεις} i. e. aspectus.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0218-N06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-notes_left-L011}{}{}{\Indent (6) Graece \textgreek{διάμετρος}.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0218-N07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-notes_left-L012}{}{}{\Indent (7) \textgreek{Τετράγωνον}; apud Latinos veteres \textit{quadra-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-notes_left-L013}{}{}{\textit{tum}.}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0218-N08}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (8) \textgreek{Τρίγωνον}; Latine \textit{trigonum, triangulum,}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-notes_right-L002}{}{}{\textit{triquetrum}.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0218-N09}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-notes_right-L003}{}{}{\Indent (9) \textgreek{Ἑξάγωνον}, \textit{hexagonum, sexangulum}.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0218-N10}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-notes_right-L004}{}{}{\Indent (10) Coniunctio aspectibus non adnumeratur;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-notes_right-L005}{}{}{cfr. parum supra l. 22, et A. \Name{Bouché-Leclercq},}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-notes_right-L006}{}{}{\textit{L'astrologie grecque}, Paris 1899, p. 165-166.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0218-N11}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-notes_right-L007}{}{}{\Indent (11) Arabice « colligata ».}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0218-N12}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-notes_right-L008}{}{}{\Indent (12) Plato 2.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0218-N13}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-notes_right-L009}{}{}{\Indent (13) Plato addit 11. — Hucusque cfr. \Name{Ptole-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-notes_right-L010}{}{}{\Name{maei} \textit{Tetrabiblum} seu \textit{Quadripartitum}, lib. I,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-notes_right-L011}{}{}{cap. 14 vulgatarum editionum (= I, 11 versionis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-notes_right-L012}{}{}{Francofurti impressae anno 1622).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0218-N14}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0218-notes_right-L013}{}{}{\Indent (14) Cfr. \textit{Tetrab.} I, 24 (Francof. I, 21).}
\end{minipage}
\par\vspace{5mm}\hfill{\fontsize{8}{10}\selectfont 17}\hspace{10mm}
\DSource{0219}{130}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0219-body-L001}{}{}{fit; cum igitur duae stellae in eadem plaga non sint vel [ab ecliptica] inaequaliter distent, con-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0219-body-L002}{}{}{iunctio in longitudine tantum, non in latitudine evenit, nec \textit{congressus corporeus} (1) recte ap-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0219-body-L003}{}{}{pellari potest; praesertim si stellae duas contrarias plagas occupent, ita ut nequaquam simul}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0219-body-L004}{}{}{conveniant. Si congressus corporeus in eadem longitudine, latitudine et plaga Caeli fit, stellae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0219-body-L005}{5}{}{remanent coniunctae eo usque quo altera ab altera dimidio amborum corporum spatio recedat (\textbf{\textit{a}}).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0219-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0219-body-L006}{}{p. 195.}{\Indent Stellae quae ad sextilem, aut quadraturam, aut trinum, aut oppositionem aliarum pro-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0219-body-L007}{}{}{grediuntur, illis \textit{applicaturae} (2) vocantur; et cum numerum [graduum aspectuum] consecutae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0219-body-L008}{}{}{sint, applicatio perficitur. Stellae leves, quae iam graves transgressae sunt, a gravibus \textit{defluen-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0219-body-L009}{}{}{\textit{tes} (3) appellantur usque quo se aliis stellis rursus applicent; si aliis se applicaturae non sint,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0219-body-L010}{10}{}{\textit{defluentes} vocantur (4).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0219-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0219-body-L011}{}{}{\Indent Quod vim (5) luminis stellarum attinet, in applicationibus dicitur vis Solis ante et retro}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0219-body-L012}{}{}{usque ad 15 (6) gradus pervenire, vis Lunae ante et retro usque ad 12°, vis Iovis ut Lunae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0219-body-L013}{}{}{12°, Veneris 8°, Martis et Mercurii et Saturni 7° (\textbf{\textit{b}}).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0219-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0219-body-L014}{}{}{\Indent Iam satis in iis quae praecedunt exposuimus vim fortiorum quantitatum, scilicet quanti-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0219-body-L015}{15}{}{tatum memoratarum in capite de corporum magnitudinibus, nec non arcus sphaerae caelestis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0219-body-L016}{}{}{quibus earum diametri subtenduntur (7); item gradus qui ab alterutro puncto solstitiali, idest}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0219-body-L017}{}{}{ab initio Cancri vel Capricorni, in anteriorem et posteriorem partem versus pariter distant,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0219-body-L018}{}{}{iam diximus se mutuo \textit{intueri} idemque \textit{valere} (8), dies enim cuiusque eorum graduum diei}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0219-body-L019}{}{}{alterius aequalis est. Decimo gradui igitur Cancri eadem vis tribuitur ac vicesimo Geminorum,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0219-body-L020}{20}{}{quia ab initio Cancri pariter distant, et diem eiusdem longitudinis habent; qua re gradus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0219-body-L021}{}{}{aequales in vi appellantur. Similiter idem valent quintus Sagittarii gradus et vicesimus quintus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0219-body-L022}{}{}{Capricorni, a Capricorni initio aeque distantes.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0219-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0219-body-L023}{}{}{\Indent Gradus qui ante et retro ab eodem puncto aequinoctiali aeque distant, \textit{imperantes} et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0219-body-L024}{}{}{\textit{obedientes} (9), vel \textit{sublimes} et \textit{humiles} vocantur. Humiles sunt gradus hemisphaerii australis,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0219-body-L025}{25}{}{scilicet ab initio Librae ad finem Piscium; sublimes gradus hemisphaerii borealis, ab initio Arietis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0219-body-L026}{}{}{ad finem Virginis; gradibus enim ab eodem puncto aequinoctiali aeque distantibus, dies gradus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0219-body-L027}{}{p. 196.}{borealis tanto longior est quanto brevior dies gradus australis. Vicesimus Piscium igitur decimo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0219-body-L028}{}{}{gradui Arietis obedit; nam dies in decimo Arietis ita crescit ut in vicesimo Piscium decrescit.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0219-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0219-body-L029}{}{}{\Indent Quantitates memoratae possunt eodem tempore in his duobus generibus reperiri et aliquem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0219-body-L030}{30}{}{aspectuum efficere; accidit enim ut initium Sagittarii se applicet initio Aquarii, et cum eo per}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0219-body-L031}{}{}{sextilem aspectum consocietur; sed cum utraque a Capricorni initio pariter distent, ambo phae-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0219-body-L032}{}{}{nomena simul habentur. Item initium Piscium potest e sextili aspectu cum initio Tauri con-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0219-body-L033}{}{}{sociari; sed initium Piscium initio Tauri obedit; ergo ambo phaenomena coniunguntur. Similia}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0219-body-L034}{}{}{evenire possunt e trino aspectu, vel quadratura, vel oppositione; ut si medium Tauri cum me-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0219-body-L035}{35}{}{dio Leonis in quadratura sit, medium Aquarii in quadratura cum medio Tauri, initium Tauri}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0219-body-L036}{}{}{in trino aspectu cum initio Virginis, initium Capricorni in trino cum initio Tauri, initium}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0219-body-L037}{}{}{Cancri in oppositione cum initio Capricorni; nam hi gradus a puncto solstitiali et a puncto}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0219-body-L038}{}{}{aequinoctiali pariter distant. Similiter initium Arietis initio Librae opponitur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0219-P06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0219-body-L039}{}{}{\Indent Planetae se stellis fixis applicant, cum inter eos distantia sextilis aspectus, trini, quadra-}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0219-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0219-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) \textgreek{Ἡ τῶν σωμάτων συναφή, σωματικῶς συνάπτειν}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0219-notes_left-L002}{}{}{(\textit{Tetrab.}).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0219-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0219-notes_left-L003}{}{}{\Indent (2) \textgreek{Συνάπτειν}; \textgreek{συναφή}, \textit{applicatio, coniunctio,}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0219-notes_left-L004}{}{}{\textit{contactus, glutinatio}. \Name{Bouché-Lecl.}, p. 245-247.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0219-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0219-notes_left-L005}{}{}{\Indent (3) \textgreek{Ἀποῤῥέοντες}; Arabice « discedentes ».}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0219-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0219-notes_left-L006}{}{}{\Indent (4) \textit{Tetrabibl.} I, 24 (Francof. I, 21).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0219-N05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0219-notes_left-L007}{}{}{\Indent (5) \textgreek{Ἐνέργεια}; cfr. \textit{Tetrab.} III, 10 (Francof. III, 14).}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0219-N06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0219-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (6) Plato 12.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0219-N07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0219-notes_right-L002}{}{}{\Indent (7) Cap. L, pag. 120-123.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0219-N08}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0219-notes_right-L003}{}{}{\Indent (8) \textgreek{Ἄλληλα βλέποντα καὶ ἰσοδυναμοῦντα μέρη},}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0219-notes_right-L004}{}{}{\textit{Tetrabibl.} I, 16 (Francof. I, 13); cfr. \Name{Bouché-Le-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0219-notes_right-L005}{}{}{\Name{clercq}, p. 159 et 161.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0219-N09}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0219-notes_right-L006}{}{}{\Indent (9) \textgreek{Προστάσσοντα καὶ ἀκούοντα μέρη}, \textit{Tetrabibl.}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0219-notes_right-L007}{}{}{I, 15 (Francof. I, 12); \Name{Bouché-Leclercq}, p. 163.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0220}{131}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0220-body-L001}{}{}{turae, oppositionis intercedat; itemque planetae et stellae fixae radios super eclipticam proii-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0220-body-L002}{}{}{ciunt diversis quantitatibus, quae maiores aut minores sunt prout latitudines differunt; nota}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0220-body-L003}{}{}{igitur distantia inter duas stellas, cognoscitur an in aliquo applicationum aspectu sint. Stellae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0220-body-L004}{}{}{fixae, ob tardum suum motum, nec per applicationem planetis nec per proiectionem radiorum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0220-body-L005}{5}{}{super eclipticam, — cum ab ea ex aliquo aspectu distent, — ad operationes [astrologicas]}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0220-body-L006}{}{}{adhibentur; sed earum aspectus in paxillis cum planetis, et corporeus congressus, praesertim}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0220-body-L007}{}{}{cum Sole, considerantur. In planetis necesse est cognoscere quantum ab alteris distent, et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0220-body-L008}{}{}{quousque super eclipticam, iuxta suas latitudines, in nativitatibus et in directione [astrologica]}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0220-body-L009}{}{}{ab altero ad alterum, radios proiiciant.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0220-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0220-body-L010}{10}{}{\Indent Manifeste oppositio perfici nequit, nisi ambae stellae in ecliptica sint, aut eandem latitu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0220-body-L011}{}{}{dinem in duabus contrariis Caeli plagis habeant. Si altera stellarum in ecliptica consistit, altera}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0220-body-L012}{}{}{vero ab ecliptica in latitudine deflectat, distantia inter eas tanto minor distantia oppositionis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0220-body-L013}{}{}{reperitur, quanta est stellae latitudo. Si ambae eandem latitudinem ab eadem parte habent,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0220-body-L014}{}{}{distantia inter eas tanto minor est longinquitate oppositionis, quanta est ambarum latitudinum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0220-body-L015}{15}{}{summa. — Quadratura quae omnibus stellis in ecliptica contingit, semper eadem est, nec a}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0220-body-L016}{}{}{90° unquam differt, sive magna sive parva est latitudo; idque in globo super cuius polos}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0220-body-L017}{}{}{circuli incidunt manifeste cognoscitur. In aspectu sextili, stella, si latitudinem habet, suos ra-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0220-body-L018}{}{p. 197.}{dios super eclipticam minus quam 60° a gradu quem ipsa occupat proiicit; in trino aspectu}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0220-body-L019}{}{}{autem longius quam 120° eadem quantitate quae in sextili deest (\textbf{\textit{c}}).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0220-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0220-body-L020}{20}{}{\Indent Si usque ad quem eclipticae gradum stella, latitudine praedita, e sextili vel trino aspectu}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0220-body-L021}{}{}{radios suos proiiciat scire vis, in tabulis sinuum inveni cosinum latitudinis, nam hic semper}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0220-body-L022}{}{}{est chorda integra secundi lateris, quod tantum in capite de diagonalibus quadrilaterorum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0220-body-L023}{}{}{[cap. XXVI] et in hoc capite ipso, in quo sumus, memoravimus. Hanc secundi lateris chor-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0220-body-L024}{}{}{dam serva. Inveni postea chordam latitudinis, quae est sinus duplicatus dimidiae latitudinis;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0220-body-L025}{25}{}{chordam servatam secundi lateris in 60 multiplica; producto adde quadratum chordae latitu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0220-body-L026}{}{}{dinis, et radicem huius summae extrahe. Quadratum quantitatis qua radix ista 60 superat,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0220-body-L027}{}{}{divide per chordam secundi lateris; quod provenit de 60 deme; restat chorda aequata. Qua-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0220-body-L028}{}{}{dratum quantitatis qua illa radix 60 superat denuo sumens, in chordam secundi lateris mul-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0220-body-L029}{}{}{tiplica; productum per chordam aequatam divide; habebis argumentum correctionis. Postea}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0220-body-L030}{30}{}{quadratum chordae latitudinis deme de 3600, idest de quadrato chordae aspectus sextilis;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0220-body-L031}{}{}{radicem residui inveni et ab ea deme argumentum correctionis; remanebit secundum latus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0220-body-L032}{}{}{aequatum. Quadratum chordae latitudinis iterum de 3600 deme; residuum divide per secun-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0220-body-L033}{}{}{dum latus aequatum; exibit chorda quaesita, quam in arcum converte, ut chordae integrae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0220-body-L034}{}{}{arcuantur, scilicet dimidiam eius partem inter sinus quaerendo, et arcum ei respondentem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0220-body-L035}{35}{}{duplicando. Arcus est quantitas aspectus sextilis stellae, a qualibet Caeli parte eius latitudo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0220-body-L036}{}{}{sit; arcum illum de 180° deme, et habebis quantitatem trini. Quam harum quantitatum vis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0220-body-L037}{}{}{de gradu [longitudinis] stellae deme eidemque adde; si Deus vult, differentia est primus locus,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0220-body-L038}{}{}{summa alter locus sextilis aut trini in ecliptica (\textbf{\textit{d}}).}
\par\vspace{4.81mm}
\par\fontsize{14.5}{17}\selectfont
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0220-H39}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0220-body-L039}{40}{p. 198.}{\CenterLine{CAPUT LV.}}
\par\vspace{2.96mm}
\par\fontsize{14.2}{16}\selectfont
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0220-H40}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0220-body-L040}{}{}{\CenterLine{De ascensionibus signorum in quadrantibus sphaerae}}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0220-H41}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0220-body-L041}{}{}{\CenterLine{qui paxillis intercipiuntur.}}
\par\fontsize{11.1}{13.8}\selectfont
\par\vspace{3.58mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0220-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0220-body-L042}{45}{}{\Indent Dicit [auctor]: Quoniam id quod [nunc] res expositas de quantitate radiorum super eclip-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0220-body-L043}{}{}{ticam [proiectorum] sequi oportet est cognitio ascensionum signorum in spatiis inter paxillos}
\DSource{0221}{132}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0221-body-L001}{}{}{— cum [hucusque] tantum cognoscantur eorum ascensiones in sphaera recta (1), quae sunt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0221-body-L002}{}{}{eorum ascensiones in paxillo medii Caeli et in paxillo Terrae (2); [itemque cognoscantur ascen-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0221-body-L003}{}{}{siones] (3) in climatibus, quae sunt signorum ascensiones occasusque in duobus horizontibus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0221-body-L004}{}{}{circuli horizontalis omnis urbis, qui sunt paxillus ascendentis et paxillus occidentis, et rerum (4)}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0221-body-L005}{5}{}{quae descripsimus quantitates variant, — id quod [nunc] ex ascensionum genere restat est}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0221-body-L006}{}{}{cognoscere ascensiones signorum in spatiis inter hos paxillos interiectos in [singulis] regionibus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0221-body-L007}{}{}{sphaerae caelestis, ut scias quanto tempore aequinoctiali fiat ortus cuiusvis signi quolibet}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0221-body-L008}{}{}{sphaerae loco, et ex eo cognoscas quantitatem temporum aequinoctialium, qui sunt inter gradum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0221-body-L009}{}{}{eclipticae anteriorem et gradum sequentem, per tempora transitus gradus anterioris eo loco (\textbf{\textit{a}}).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0221-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0221-body-L010}{10}{}{\Indent Rei exemplum praebebimus: totum Arietis signum in paxillo medii Caeli cum 27° 53′ (5)}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0221-body-L011}{}{}{temporum aequinoctialium ascendit, et eadem cum quantitate transit per paxillum Terrae; in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0221-body-L012}{}{}{quarto climate cum 19° 12′ temporum aequinoctialium ascendit, et cum quantitate ortus Librae,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0221-body-L013}{}{}{scilicet cum 36° 34′ occidit. Inter unumquemque horum paxillorum et paxillum sequentem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0221-body-L014}{}{}{6 horae temporales, scilicet horae unius ex quartis partibus diei et noctis habentur; quae,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0221-body-L015}{15}{}{si in duabus quartis partibus super Terram sunt, diurnae esse debent, at si in reliquis quartis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0221-body-L016}{}{}{sub Terra, nocturnae. Declinante Ariete ab aliquo horum paxillorum, quantitates eius ascen-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0221-body-L017}{}{}{sionum variant, nam crescunt aut decrescunt ad numerum horarum temporalium quibus ini-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0221-body-L018}{}{}{tium Arietis a paxillo dato distat. Ponamus primo initium Arietis a medio Caelo in orientem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0221-body-L019}{}{}{versus duabus horis temporalibus abesse; tunc ascensiones Arietis erunt minores quam ascen-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0221-body-L020}{20}{}{siones in medio Caeli quantitate 2° 54′ (6), quae sunt tertia pars differentiae inter ascensiones}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0221-body-L021}{}{}{Arietis in medio Caelo et ascensiones eius in climate in horizonte orientali, sicut duae horae,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0221-body-L022}{}{}{quibus a medio Caelo distat, sunt tertia pars sex horarum inter medium Caelum et ascendens}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0221-body-L023}{}{}{intercedentium. — Si Arietis initium a medio Caelo in eandem partem versus tribus horis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0221-body-L024}{}{p. 199}{temporalibus distaret, eius ortus tunc minor ortu in sphaera recta esset quantitate 4° 20′, quae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0221-body-L025}{25}{}{sunt dimidia longinquitas ascensionis rectae ab ascensione in horizonte climatis. — Cum autem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0221-body-L026}{}{}{ad ascendens pervenerit, ascensio Arietis tunc minor quam ascensio recta erit 8° 41′ (7), quae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0221-body-L027}{}{}{est differentia tota inter ea.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0221-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0221-body-L028}{}{}{\Indent Ponamus quoque initium Arietis a medio Caelo in occidentem versus duabus horis tem-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0221-body-L029}{}{}{poralibus distare; cum occasus eius in hemisphaerio occidentali sint ut ascensiones Librae,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0221-body-L030}{30}{}{occasus eius tunc superabunt ascensiones rectas tertia parte huius differentiae, scilicet 2° 54′ (8).}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0221-body-L031}{}{}{Si in eandem partem versus initium Arietis tribus horis distat, eius transitus illac est dimidia}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0221-body-L032}{}{}{parte totius differentiae, 4° 20′ scilicet, maior quam transitus per sphaeram rectam. Si demum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0221-body-L033}{}{}{ad horizontem occidentalem pervenit, eius occasus superat eius ortum in sphaera recta diffe-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0221-body-L034}{}{}{rentia tota, idest 8° 41′ (9). — Similiter si a paxillo Terrae in ascendens versus distat, res se}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0221-body-L035}{35}{}{habet ut inter medium Caelum et ascendens; si autem a paxillo Terrae in occidentem versus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0221-body-L036}{}{}{abest, ut inter medium Caelum et occasum res evenit.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0221-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0221-body-L037}{}{}{\Indent Si igitur ascensiones cuiuslibet gradus quacumque sphaerae regione scire vis, ante omnia}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0221-body-L038}{}{}{distantiam gradus vel sideris dati ab aliquo paxillorum deprehende ut nunc dicam: stella lati-}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0221-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0221-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) De quibus in V capite disseritur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0221-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0221-notes_left-L002}{}{}{\Indent (2) Signa enim per circulum meridianum cu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0221-notes_left-L003}{}{}{iusque Terrae loci transeunt cum eadem circuli aequi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0221-notes_left-L004}{}{}{noctialis quantitate quae est eorum ascensio recta;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0221-notes_left-L005}{}{}{v. pag. 13, l. 36-37, et pag. 27, l. 9-10.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0221-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0221-notes_left-L006}{}{}{\Indent (3) In textu Arabico, qui haud parum est ob-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0221-notes_left-L007}{}{}{scurus, p. 198, l. 5 pro \ArabicRun{في الاقاليم} codicis legatur \ArabicRun{وفي}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0221-notes_left-L008}{}{}{\ArabicRun{الاقاليم}. Platonis versio nullum sensum praebet. —}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0221-notes_right-L001}{}{}{De ascensionibus obliquis iam disseruit auctor in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0221-notes_right-L002}{}{}{XIII capite, pag. 27-29.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0221-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0221-notes_right-L003}{}{}{\Indent (4) I. e. ascensionum obliquarum.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0221-N05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0221-notes_right-L004}{}{}{\Indent (5) Plato 27° 23′.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0221-N06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0221-notes_right-L005}{}{}{\Indent (6) Plato 2° 24′.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0221-N07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0221-notes_right-L006}{}{}{\Indent (7) Plato 8° 11′.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0221-N08}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0221-notes_right-L007}{}{}{\Indent (8) Plato 2° 24′.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0221-N09}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0221-notes_right-L008}{}{}{\Indent (9) Plato 8° 11′.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0222}{133}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0222-body-L001}{}{}{tudine carens se ut gradus eclipticae quem occupat gerit; qua re tempora horarum diurna-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0222-body-L002}{}{}{rum et nocturnarum illius gradus cognosce. Sed si stella latitudinem habet, gradum cum ea}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0222-body-L003}{}{}{culminantem, et tempora horaria gradus super Terram et sub Terra deprehende; sextam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0222-body-L004}{}{}{partem dimidiae morae super Terram sumendo, ut horas super Terram habeas, eundemque}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0222-body-L005}{5}{}{sextantem de 30 demendo, ut exeant horae sub Terra (1) [i. e. nocturnae], sicut in anteriore}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0222-body-L006}{}{}{parte libri (2) iam exposuimus. Pro gradu [longitudinis] quem stella occupat, utere gradu}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0222-body-L007}{}{}{cum stella culminanti, [si stella latitudinem habet;] si vero latitudine caret, [adhibe gradum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0222-body-L008}{}{}{longitudinis stellae,] et similiter pro temporibus horariis gradus cum ea culminantis (3) tem-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0222-body-L009}{}{}{pora horaria stellae delige. Si gradus [longitudinis] adhibendus consistit super Terram, sume}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0222-body-L010}{10}{}{eius distantiam a medio Caelo, in ascensionibus rectis; si autem sub Terra est, accipe eius}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0222-body-L011}{}{}{distantiam a paxillo Terrae, per ascensiones rectas quoque; idest ascensiones gradus medii}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0222-body-L012}{}{p. 200.}{Caeli de ascensionibus gradus quo uteris deme, si hic ab oriente medii Caeli consistit; aut ab}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0222-body-L013}{}{}{ascensionibus gradus medii Caeli detrahe ascensiones gradus adhibendi, si hic ab occidente}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0222-body-L014}{}{}{reperitur; et similiter cum gradu paxilli Terrae operare. Tempora distantiae provenientia divide}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0222-body-L015}{15}{}{per tempora horaria diei, si gradus adhibitus super Terram est, aut per tempora horaria no-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0222-body-L016}{}{}{ctis, si gradus tuus est sub Terra; exibit stellae vel gradus distantia a paxillo medii Caeli}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0222-body-L017}{}{}{vel a paxillo Terrae.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0222-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0222-body-L018}{}{}{\Indent Utrum gradus vel stella super Terram an sub ea reperiantur, ut nunc dicam cognosci-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0222-body-L019}{}{}{tur: si gradus quo uti debes est inter ascendens et gradum occidentem ad successionem signo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0222-body-L020}{20}{}{rum, gradus ille sub Terra consistit; si autem inter gradum occidentem et ascendens, ad suc-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0222-body-L021}{}{}{cessionem signorum, reperitur, super Terram est. Id aliter quoque deprehenditur: si dimidia}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0222-body-L022}{}{}{stellae mora super Terram est maior temporibus inter gradum medii Caeli et gradum cum stella}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0222-body-L023}{}{}{in sphaera recta culminantem intercedentibus, stella super Terram consistit; si minor, sub Terra.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0222-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0222-body-L024}{}{}{\Indent Horis temporalibus cognitis, quibus stella vel gradus a medio Caelo vel a paxillo Terrae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0222-body-L025}{25}{}{distat, si vis eius distantiam ab ascendente vel ab occidente cognoscere, horas illas de 6 deme;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0222-body-L026}{}{}{residuum est longinquitas a paxillo ascendentis aut occidentis. Qua re si ascensiones cuiuslibet}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0222-body-L027}{}{}{gradus in loco sphaerae quem tenet scire cupis, ob res expositas tempora aequatoris inter}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0222-body-L028}{}{}{gradum eclipticae anteriorem et gradum sequentem intercedentia manifeste possunt deprehendi}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0222-body-L029}{}{}{eodem modo quo ascensiones obliquae et rectae inter duos gradus cognoscuntur. Idest quan-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0222-body-L030}{30}{}{tum aequatoris tempus necessarium sit ut gradus sequens eclipticae perveniat ad locum quem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0222-body-L031}{}{}{gradus anterior occupabat, deprehende; si gradus anterior datus inter medium Caelum et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0222-body-L032}{}{}{paxillum Terrae ad orientem versus consistit, in hemisphaerio orientali est; si vero inter me-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0222-body-L033}{}{}{dium Caelum et paxillum Terrae ad occasum, in hemisphaerio occidentali suum locum tenet.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0222-body-L034}{}{p. 201.}{Cum eum in hemisphaerio orientali scias, horas temporales quibus distat a medio Caelo, vel}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0222-body-L035}{35}{}{a paxillo Terrae, vel ab ascendente, prout mavis, cognosce; postea, si etiam gradus sequens}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0222-body-L036}{}{}{hemisphaerium orientale tenet, quaere ascensiones rectas et ascensiones obliquas inter duos}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0222-body-L037}{}{}{gradus illos. Si duo numeri aequales proveniunt, ipsi sunt tempora aequatoris inter utrosque}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0222-body-L038}{}{}{gradus intercedentia; si diversi, minorem de maiore deme, et sextam partem residui, quae est}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0222-body-L039}{}{}{pars ex ea differentia proportionalis ad unam horam, multiplica in horas distantiae gradus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0222-body-L040}{40}{}{praecedentis a paxillo antea constituto. Si horis distantiae a medio Caelo vel paxillo Terrae}
